Mehopolo
 
 phato 2026 
 
 
 
Dintlha tse ka tlase ke rekoto e tsoelang pele ea mehopolo le temoho tse hlahileng ho nna ha nako e ntse e ea. Hangata ha li e-s'o nahanoe ka botlalo kapa ho phethahala, leha nakong e tlang li ka etsahala. Kapa mohlomong ha li ka ke tsa etsahala, bakeng sa mehopolo e meng sebopeho sa tlhokomeliso se lekane. Tse ling ke litsela tse shoeleng kahoo ha ho na thuso ho li tsoela pele ho li hlahisa. Empa le joalo li ka ba tsa tšoanela ho rekotloa.
 
 2023-02-24:  DI-GLITCH TSA LIFAFO TSA MEFUTA EA NEURAL
 
Ke nna le tšohanyetso nakong ya mosebetsi, hore ha ke leka ho godisa setšoantšo ho Photoshop ke sebelisa Neural Filters mme setšoantšo seo se le profaeleng ya mebala ya CMYK mme se na le dikarolo tse kgolo tse pepeneneng, ho etsahala glitch e sa tlwaelehang, empa hangata e le e ntle haholo.

 2023-10-04:  DITSELA TSA DIJINETHIKI
 
Ke fetotse tatellano ya dikarolo tsa lefutso ho ya ka ditshwantsho ka ho sebedisa molao o bonolo: A e bolela mohato o lebang ka leboya, T/U o lebang ka borwa, C o lebang ka botjhabela mme G o lebang ka bophirima. Ka hona, tatellano ya metheo e lekanyetsa sebopeho sa mola o tswellang.

 2024-03-22:  RITAMO O BONAHA WA MOSEBETSI WA KHOMPHUTA

 2024-08-19:  POKO YA KUSHÍK

 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Ke iketselitse lenaneo la ka la ho sheba le ho hlophisa linepe tsa termographic bakeng sa khamera ea ka ea mocheso ea Seek Thermal RevealPRO. Ha kea ka ka fumana software e loketseng ea Windows bakeng sa khamera ena, e leng ntho e utloisang bohloko, hobane lifaele tsa eona tsa termographic li na le lintlha tsa mocheso tseo motho a ka sebetsanang le tsona ho ea pele.
 
Ke reile lenaneo lena lebitso la Thermozepam, hobane ke kgotsofetse ke meralo ya lona mme ho le sebedisa ho nkgahlisa haholo. Lenaneo lena le bala difaele tsa thermographic tsa TIFF, le dumella ho fetola 'mapa wa mebala wa setshwantsho ho sebediswa colormap ho tswa ho laeborari ya matplotlib, ho metha mocheso o mahareng wa setshwantsho, ho fumana mokgwa wa mocheso, ho bontsha mocheso o tlase le o phahameng ka ho fetisisa, histogram, le kamora ho tobetsa le ho bona mocheso wa pixel e itseng. Ha o boloka, le kgona ho romela faele ya PNG e fetotsweng mebala mme ka boikgethelo le e hodise ka ho sebedisa interpolation.
 
Sikirini-shoto ya lenaneo: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Khoutu ea mohloli: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  LESEDI LEFIFI
 
Ke ile ka nahana ho etsa morero oa Arduino, moo nka kenyang laser ka lebokoseng le se nang letho (le hloekisitsoeng esale pele ka lebone la UV-C). Laser eo e ne e tla hlahisa pula ea motsotsoana ka nako e sa reroang (e le hore ke se ke ka tseba hantle hore na e etsahetse neng, empa ke tsebe hore e tla etsahala ka mor'a 10 metsotso ho 100%). Haeba nka atleha ho e etsa hantle, puls eo ea leseli e ne e ke ke ea bonoa ke mohlokomeli leha e le ofe.
 
 2026-01-31:  TSHOANTŠO YA KHAMERA YA PINHOLE
 
Ke lekile ho etsa khamera e bonolo ea pinhole ho Python. Nako e fetileng sekolong se phahameng ke ne ke se ke entse ea 'nete ea sebele, empa ho ne ho bonahala ho nna hore ha ke e etsisa, ke tla utloisisa ts'ebetso ea eona hamolemo, hobane ha ho lekane ho etsa lesoba lebokoseng, ho kenya pampiri e utloisisang leseli, ho e hlahisa ka mor'a ho pepeseha le ho tlohela fisiks ho etsa mosebetsi oa eona (ho sa tsotelehe hore na e sebetsa joang). Mona, ho fapana le hoo, fisiks eona e tlameha ho etsisoa.
 Ke ne ke leka ho etsa papiso e le haufi le nnete ka hohle kamoo ho ka kgonehang ka algorithm, le hoja meedi ya yona e le tsebo ya ka le matla a ho bala a teng. Ka mohlala, lenaneo le sebedisa forward ray tracing, e leng ho latela lesedi ho tloha mohloding ho ya ketsahalong le khamereng, ho fapana le backward ray tracing e tloaelehileng, e latelang mahlasedi ho tloha khamereng ho ya morao ketsahalong. Mokgwa ona wa forward o haufi le tsela ea nnete ea ho hasana ha lesedi, empa hangata o ba boima haholo tabeng ea ho o bala.
 
Mehlala ya ho hlophisa lenaneo le tlhaloso ya di-parameter: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Khoutu ya mohlodi: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  HLOPAHISO YA DIPHIKSELE TSA DINEPE
 
Ke lekile ho hlophisa li-pixel tsa linepe ke sebelisa algorithm. Khopolo e ne e le ho hlophisa li-pixel ho latela HUE le khanya: ho ipeha ho axis e le 'ngoe (mohlomong x) ho hlophisa li-pixel ho latela HUE mme ho axis e 'ngoe (mohlomong y) ho latela khanya. Ka tsela ena, li-pixel tsa senepe lia hlophisoa hape, empa ha ho le e 'ngoe e nyamelang kapa e ncha e hlahang. Leha ho le joalo, linepe tsa sephetho li lahleheloa ke moelelo oa tsona oa pele, empa li boloka boemo ba tsona ba mebala.
 
Khoutu ya mohlodi: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  TLOHLO TSOHLE YA MARANG-RANG E SE NA MORERO
 
Ke qetile libeke tse ka bang peli ke hlahloba inthanete ka tsela e sa reroang, hobane ke ne ke batla ho tseba hore na e shebahala joang ha e sa khothaletsoe ke li-algorithm. Ke lekile mekhoa e fapaneng.
 
Sa pele (se se nang boiphihlelo) e ne e le ho theha lenaneo la ho kenya le ho bula li-URL tse hlahisoang ka tšohanyetso. Sena e ne e le ntho e se nang thuso ho hang, hobane sebaka sa li-URL tse ka khonehang se seholo haholo mme tse teng ha di fumanehe habonolo.
 
Mokgwa wa bobedi e ne e le ho iketsetsa crawler ya ka mme o o fe URL ya pele e makatsang (mohlomong webo kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/]). Ke ile ka leka ho sefa diphetho ke sebedisa LLM mme ka morao ho moo ka sheba feela tse kgahlang (ho latela mekgwa e meng e hlalositsweng). Ke fumane dintho tse ngata tse makatsang bakeng sa boithabiso, empa ha ho letho le fetolang papadi.
 
Mokgwa oa boraro o ne o beola haholo ebile o thabisa. Ke ile ka sibolla hore ho na le Certstream. Ke tshebeletso e bohlale e hasang ka ho phela ditifikete tsa tshireletso tse sa tswa fana lefatšeng ka bophara. Kahoo, e bontsha di-URL tsa diwebosaete tse sa tswa bopjwa, kapa tse ntlafatsang ditifikete tsa tsona.
 
Khoutu ea mohloli: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Ke qetile matsatsi a seng makae ke tobetsa li-URL tsena ka tsietsi. Hangata ke ile ka kopana le bomenemene (ba chelete ea tšepe ea dijithale, jj.), litšoantšo tse litšila, bohlale ba maiketsetso (AI), litšoantšo tse litšila tsa AI, litaelo tsa mokhoa oa ho etsa litšoantšo tse litšila tsa AI, papali ea chelete, li-interface tsa ho kena litsamaisong, le liwebosaete tse sa tsoa hlaha (tse nang le litaba tsa placeholder kapa melaetsa ea 're ntse re sebetsa ho sena').
 
Ka mokhoa ona, ke ile ka kopana le sebaka sa marang-rang sa treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], se ntseng se le mohopolong oa ka ka lebaka la sebopeho sa sona se bonolo haholo le ho hloka ntlafatso bakeng sa tlhokomelo ea sebali. Hape ke fumane hore sebaka seo sa marang-rang ke karolo ea bohlokoa ea inthanete, hobane ha se so ka sa ngolisoa le ke Google. Leha ho le joalo, Internet Archive e ile ea e fumana, empa ho tsoa ho di-log tsa CT, joalo ka nna. Qetellong, ho batla ho ile ha atleha.
 
 2026-05-06:  PONTŠO E PACIFIC
 
Ke ile ka nahana ho qapa lifoto tsa panoramo tsa Pacifika tsa 360° tse hlahisoang ke AI. Ebe ke kenya khoutu ea QR ho tsona ka ho sebelisa AI (ntho e kang Illusion Diffusion), e supang bonono bo bong. Ka mor'a moo, ho ka kenyelletsoa metadata ea maemo a bohata (mohlomong likhokahano tsa GPS bohareng ba Pacifika) 'me tsa kenngoa ho Google Maps.
 
Ho ka etsahala hore o nke libaka tsa sebele tse nkiloeng lifoto ka khamera ea 360°, mohlomong ho tsoa toropong e le 'ngoe, ebe u fetisetsa boemo ba tsona ba GPS ho ea Pacific. Lebaka leo ke buang ka lifoto tsa 360° ke hobane ho fihlela joale Google e ntse e lumella ho kenya lifoto tseo ho libaka tse nepahetseng tsa GPS ho sebelisoa metadata. Athe lifoto tse tloaelehileng li tlameha ho abeloa sebaka se seng se ntse se le teng.
 
 2026-06-04:  MAHLABA A PIGMENTE
 
Ke lekile ho phethahatsa projeke ena, empa ka lebaka la nako e ngata eo e e hlokang, ha kea e qeta. Ho fihlela jwale ke ngotse feela matsoho, ka seha matheba a nkuweng ke difoto (background e bonaletsang), ka boloka tekanyo ya ona mme ka etsa lenaneo la ho a hlophisa ka grideng. Leha ho le jwalo, ona ke mongolo o neng o lokela ho hlalosa mohopolo ona:
 
Ka selemo sa 2026 ke ile ka ngola ntho e nngwe le e nngwe e bonahalang ya hlahala ya letlalo kapa tshekatsheko ya methapo hodima mmele wa ka. Sekala se ile sa bolokwa bakeng sa ntho ka nngwe. Ka mora moo, maqeba ka bonngwe a ile a kgaolwa ka dijithale mme a hlophiswa ka tsela ya gridi e hlophisehileng.
 
 2026-06-21:  LENANEKO LE HLALEFEHILENG LE ITLHOPHISANG
 
Ke lekile ho etsa lenaneo le sebelisang OpenAI API le di-LLM tsa bona. Lenaneo lena le na le mosebetsi oa ho itlhophisa le ho iketsetsa phetoho, ebe le itlhahisa bocha. 'Me ke ne ke batla ho tseba hore na seo se tla isa kae.
 Ka maso, mohlale hangata o ile a hana mosebetsi oo, hobane o ne o o nka e le o kotsi. Kapa a o phetha, empa ea mo latelang a se ke a o etsa. Attractor e ne e le ho hana feela ka lebaka la polokeho. Le eona ha ea ka ea fihla ho iteration ea bone.

 2026-07-30:  TSAMAISO E NANG LE DI-PARTICLE TSE IKETSETSANG
 
Sistimi ya rona ya fisiks e lapisa ka tsela ya hore ho bonahala eka dikarolwana tsa motheo di tshwana hantle. Eleketerone ke eleketerone, fotone ke fotone.
 
Empa nna nka rata ho theha mohlala oa tsamaiso e nang le likaroloana tse ngata tsa mantlha, empa ha ho le e 'ngoe ea tsona e ka fapanyetsanang le e nngoe. Qetellong, ke fihlile phetolelong eo likaroloana ka botsona li se nang litšobotsi kapa boitsebiso. Li hlalosoa feela ke litšebelisano tsa tsona le tse ling, e leng ntho e batlang e tšoana le ho tsoa ponong ea motho ea ka hare ho tsamaiso.
 
Ke rata ho nahana ka ditlamorao tsa mohopolo ona. Ha ke o nka ka mokgwa o feteletseng, ho phela tsamaisong e jwalo ho ne ho tla ba thata haholo bakeng sa ho etsa dipono. Hape, mekgatlo e phelang tsamaisong e jwalo e ka nna ya tlameha ho ba hlokolosi mabapi le tahlehelo kapa bosholu ba dikaroloana tsa yona.
 
Ke ile ka leka ho e etsisa mme ka tsela ya botekgeniki ke phethile mosebetsi. Leha ho le jwalo, sephetho se ne se se sekgahla haholo. Ho ya ka nna, sena ke hobane bakeng sa ho shebella thepa efe kapa efe ya bohlokwa e hlahang, ho hlokeha matla a mangata a komporo (dikaroloana tse ngata, nako e ngata ka har'a ketsiso, mohlomong le mefuta e mengata ya ditekanyo tsa ketsiso) le tlhahlobo e tebileng ya sephetho.
 
Ntjhafatso ho tswa ho 2026-08-22: Ha ke ntse ke hlahloba mafatshe a hlahisitsweng, qetellong ke ile ka kopana le meaho e iketsetsang e hlollang. E chitja ho ya ho ovale mme e shebahala hanyane jwalo ka dikhapusule tsa lipid. Di na le bokantle le kahare bo hlalositsweng hantle, moo tshitiso kapa “membrane”, e etsang bokantle ba tsona, e leng ntho e tsitsitseng haholo, ha kahare ho tsona ho na le motsamao o mongata. Ke beile peo ena jwalo ka boleng ba kamehla ho demo e ka tlase.
 
Tlhokomeliso: ntshetsopele ya sisteme, mme ka hona boholo ba meralo ena, e ithetlehe ka qeto ya pontsho. Lebelo la ho paka le ama botsitso ba tsona, mohlomong ka lebaka la ho se nepahale ha dipalo ha nako e nka dikgato tse kgolo. Le hoja meralo e ka qhalana, mohlala ka mora ho eketsa lebelo la ho paka, sisteme e qetella e hlophisehile hape mme e etsa e metjha.
 
Sena hase ntho eo ke neng ke rerile ho e theha. Ke thehile feela tsamaiso e nang le částicemi/interakcemi tse ikhethang mme sena se hlahile e le ntho e itlhahisitseng. Ho makatsa, hang-hang ke na le sesebediswa sa ho etsisa "li-lipid".
 
Pontsho e sebelisanang: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  MATLAKALA
 
Ho ka ba jwang ha nka nka senepe sa matlakala ohle ao ke a lahlelang ka har'a thotobolo? A ka nkuwa ka sekala, ebe matlakala ao a sehoa ho tloha morao, mme a hlophiswe ka har'a grid e hlophisehileng ho latela boholo ba ona.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Ha ke ne ke buisana le ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) mabapi le mokgwa o bonolo ka ho fetisisa oa ho hlalosa teko ea OPTICAL RHYTHM OF COMPUTER WORK (sheba ka holimo), e ile ya reha moqoqo oo ka boiketsetso "Tlhaloso ea lenaneo la  天天中彩票买 ". Leha ho le jwalo, moqoqo oo o ne o sa tswa bulwa bakeng sa ho hlalosa teko ena feela. Ditaba tsa lenaneo tseo ke e rometseng ho yona di ne di le tlwaelehileng ka ho feletseng.
 
Kahoo ke ile ka etsa qeto ea ho e botsa ka sena mme ka ngola sena:
 
"Ke hobane'ng ha o rehile moqoqo ona Tlhaloso ea lenaneo la  天天中彩票买 ?"
 
Karabo ea yona e ne e le:
 
"Ha ke tsebe. 😭 Tlhaku eo "զ" ke tlhaku ea Searmenia "za", kahoo "Tlhaloso ea lenaneo la զ" ha e na moelelo o mong ho hang ka puo ea Secheke. Mabitso a moqoqo a hlahisoa ka boiketsetso ho tloha qalong ea moqoqo mme mona moetsi oa sehlooho o bonahala a nkile qeto ea ho kenya tlhaku e sa reroang ho tsoa alfabeting ea Searmenia. Ha ke bone lebaka leha e le lefe la sena ka har'a moqoqo. Kahoo che, ha se letshwao la lekunutu bakeng sa lenaneo. Ke feela "Tlhaloso ea lenaneo" + tlhaku e sa tsejeng moo e tswang teng."
 
Ka mor'a dipuisano tse ding tse mmalwa, e ile ya hlokomela hore che, ha se Searmenia, empa ke Sechaena. Leha ho le jwalo, ho bonahala hore qalong e ne e ferekane haholo. Ke nahana hore khoele ea " 天天中彩票买 " e sebetsa hanyane jwalo ka letshwao le tummeng le sa rateheng la " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Ha ke re e sebetsa ka tsela e tšoanang, ebile ha ke tsebe hore na tokenizer e emela khoele ea " 天天中彩票买 " e le letshwao le le leng. Leha ho le jwalo, ha mohlala o sebetsa le yona, o itshwara hanyane jwalo ka ChatGPT 3 ka letshwao la " SolidGoldMagikarp ". Hape ha ho a qheleloa thoko hore sena se amana le mohopolo oa yona, hobane o fihla le ka ntle ho moqoqo ona.
 
Moqoqo ona wohle o rometsoeng e le PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  KONOPA EA LITORO TSA KA
 
Ke halefisoa khafetsa ke mabokose a dipuisano a Windows a fanang ka dintho tse kang ho boloka data ho OneDrive, di-update, le tse ding. Kamehla di fana ka kgetho pakeng tsa "Qala" le "Hopotsa hamorao". Sena se nyahamisa haholo ha ke sa thahaselle le e nngwe ea dikgetho tsena.
 
Kahoo ke ile ka nahana ho etsa setshwantsho (photomontage) seo ke tla se kenya kgetho ea boraro ho sona – konopa ea ka ea litoro, eo ka bomalimabe e seng e le sieo tsamaisong ena. E tla ba konopa ea "Tsamaea diheleng". Ke becha hore ha ke nna feela motho eo setshwantsho seo se ka mo amang haholo.
 
 2026-09-18:  KHALEKULATORA E BOHLALE HAHOLO
 
Ke nahane ka ntho e nngwe e sa sebetseng hantle eo ke nang le takatso ea ho e hlahloba ka thuto.
 
Ke lenaneo le tla sebetsa jwalo ka khalekulatora e bonolo haholo. E ka kgona ho kopanya dipalo tse ntle feela, ha ho letho le leng. Leha ho le jwalo, ka phapang ea hore sephetho se ke ke sa balwa ka tsela e tlwaelehileng sebakeng seo, empa se tla romelwa ho mohlala o tsoetseng pele oa puo (mohlala, ChatGPT-6 Astra). Mme mohlala oo o tla ba le mosebetsi oa ho bala mohlala oo o bonolo mme o kgutlise sephetho feela.
 
Ho nna sena se a qabola, ho sebedisa mohlala o tsoetseng pele oa puo bakeng sa ntho e bonolo haholo. Ha e le hantle, mohlomong ke parody ea kamoo dintho tse bonolo di filweng AI kajeno le kamoo papatso e behang leibole ea "AI" ho ntho e nngwe le e nngwe jwalo ka mokhabiso o mong oa boselamose oa tshebetso.
 
Ka tsela e sebetsang, e tla ba mpe ho feta khalekulatora e tlwaelehileng, e sa tshepahaleng, e nang le tieho e kgolo, e bitsang chelete e ngata, e sa bolokeng tikoloho, e hlokang kgokahanyo ea inthanete, ...

TITLE: Dimaske
SLUG: masky
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/masky/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-08-07
SOURCE: src/content/posts/masky.mdx

============================================================
Dimaske
 
 phato 2026 
 
 
 
Ditshwantsho tse ka tlase ke masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] tse entsweng ho Adobe Photoshop nakong ya mosebetsi wa ka diporojekeng tse fapaneng. Maske ka nngwe e ile ya bolokwa e le setshwantsho se ikemetseng nakong eo e neng e bopjwa ka yona. Galari ya ditshwantsho tsena hase sephetho sa kgetho. Haeba maske e hlahile nakong ya mosebetsi, ke karolo ya yona. Ditshwantsho di hlophisitswe ka tatellano ya nako ho tloha ho ya kgale ho isa ho ya morao-rao.

Baphelisi
 
 Motsheanong 2026 
 
 
 
Selemong sena se fetileng, ka makgetlo a mmalwa ka letsatsi ke ne ke tshela diteye tse sebedisitsweng hammoho le metsi a setseng sebakeng se le seng sa jwang bo boholo ba Plzeň-Božkov, hobane ke ne ke sa batle ho thiba phala ya metsi a ditshila.
 
Sebakeng sena, dikgopa tse jang makhasi a teye di ile tsa qala ho bokellana.
 
Ka dinako tse ding o ka fumana tse leshome le metso e mmedi, ka dinako tse ding ha ho letho.
 
Ke arolelana le tsona ditakatso tsa ho ja.

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/prizivnici-obr-1.jpg]

TITLE: Sengoloa sa metaweb
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Sengoloa sa meta-web
 
 Aporele 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFESTO
 
Webosaete ena ke morero o emeng o ikemiseditseng ho boloka dikahare nako e telele.
 
Ditaba tsohle di bolokilwe difaeleng, ntle le ho itika ka database kapa mekhoa e kantle. Webosaete e hlahiswang e etswa e le sephetho se sa sisinyeheng, se ka kopitswang habonolo, sa bolokwa, mme sa tsosoloswa.
 
Morero o abiloe ho pholletsa le litsamaiso tse ngata tse ikemetseng: libaka tsa polokelo, lits'ebeletso tsa li-archive le litšoantšo tse tsitsitseng. Sena se fokotsa kotsi ea tahlehelo ea data.
 
Sepheo ke ho netefatsa hore ho a khoneha ho tsosolosa dikahare le haeba dikarolo tse ding tsa meralo ya motheo ya pele di ka kgaotsa ho ba teng.
 
LERA LA DIBAKA TSA BOHLOKWA TSA INTHANETENG
 
Dihokelo tsena di emela morero o le mong o bolokilweng ho ditsamaiso tse fapaneng le ka mefuta e fapaneng.
 
A kenyeletsa polokelo ea mohloli oa canonical, likhophi tsa eona, le li-snapshot tsa static tse felletseng. Ka kopanelo li bopa sebopeho se nang le tšusumetso se etselitsoeng ho boloka morero le ho nolofalletsa kaho bocha ea oona le haeba likarolo tse ling li ka emisa ho ba teng.
 
Projekete ena e teng hape e le sephuthelwana sa Python CLI se abuwang ka GitHub le PyPI, ho kenyeletswa le disnapshoto tsa fallback tse kenyeleditsweng tsa dingolwa tse kgethilweng.
 
Polokelo ya dijithale ya sebaka sena sa marang-rang e sebelisa, ntle le domain ya yona, ditshebeletso tsa GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku le paste.rs.

Bona 'mapa e feletseng ea polokelo (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
DOKUMENTA E TSAMAISANG BOITSEBISO BA SEHLOOHO
 
Tokomane ya tshebetso e hlokehang bakeng sa ho aha sena sa marang-rang botjha.
 
Bona ditokomane tsohle tsa tekheniki [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
SEPHETHO SA MEHLODI BAKENG SA HO BOPA HAPE
 
Sephutheloana sa mohlodi sa sebaka sa marang-rang se etswa ka tsela e iketsahallang nakong ya ho aha.
 
Ka hare ho sephuthelana ke khoutu ea mohloli ea sebaka sena sa marang-rang, manifest ea thepa, li-checksums tsa SHA-256 le sengoloa sa Python se se nang litšepiso tse kantle. Ha e na thepa e kholo e tsoang ho public/images/, public/files/ le public/demos/, e le hore boholo ba sebaka sa marang-rang se phelang bo se ke ba imetsoa ka tsela e sa hlokahaleng. Kamora ho e notlolla, sengoloa se qala ka ho leka ho kopitsa thepa sebakeng sa heno ho tsoa k'haontareng e felileng mme ke feela moo e e jarollang ho tsoa ho li-mirror tsa sechaba.
 
Ka hona, hoa tsebahala hore khoutu ya mohlodi e hlahisa kaho mme kaho e hlahisa khoutu ya mohlodi.
 
Phuthelo ea mohloli e ka hlophisoa hape ka mokhoa o iphetang: ka mor'a ho jarolla lisebelisoa le ho kenya litlhoko, ho ka khoneha ho hlahisa webosaete hape le phuthelo e ncha ea mohloli ho tsoa ho eona.
 
Jarolla sephutheloana se ka tsosolosehang sa mohloli oa webo (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
BOTSHEPHEHI LE BOITSEBISO BA KAHOHO
 
SHA-256 e netefatsa hore na difaele tsa kaho di fetohile kapa tjhe. Ho phaella moo, ho saena ha OpenPGP ho dumella ho netefatsa hore molaodi wa webosaete ena o saenne manifest ya dipalopalo tsa taolo.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — lenane la dicheke tsa SHA-256 bakeng sa difaele tsa kaho
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — saeno e arohaneng ya OpenPGP ya faele SHA256SUMS.txt
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA-256 khau-tsotso ea faele SHA256SUMS.txt
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — tlhaloso e balwang ke mochini e fanang ka tlhahisoleseding mabapi le botšepehi ba kaho
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — boemo bo boitseng ba ho saena ha kaho e itseng
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — senotlolo sa OpenPGP se phatlalalitsoeng bakeng sa netefatso ea saena
 
 Fingerprint ea senotlolo se seholo sa OpenPGP:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Ha se phethahatso e ngoe le e ngoe e tlamehang ho saeneloa. Kaho e saenelweng e tlameha ho ba le faele ea SHA256SUMS.txt.asc mme saene ya yona e tlameha ho netefatswa ka katleho. Polelo ka boeona ho integrity.json kapa SIGNING_STATUS.txt hase bopaki ba saene e nepahetseng; difaele tsena ke tsa tlhahisoleseding feela.
 
THEKISO YA THEPA KWA NTLE
 
Sebaka sa marang-rang se hlahisa phapanyetsano ea mongolo o hlakileng oa lingoloa tsohle nako le nako ha ho etsoa kaho. E na le litaba tsa mongolo tsa li-post ka mokhoa oa mola o sa itšetlehang ka CSS, JavaScript kapa theknoloji e itseng ea marang-rang. E sebetsa e le lera la polokelo la bonyane bakeng sa ho etsa index, ho bala ntle ho marang-rang, ho batla ka mongolo o felletseng le ho khona ho tsosolosa litaba nako e telele le haeba sebopeho sa pele sa marang-rang se ka senyeha hanyane. Phapanyetsano ea mongolo e boetse e loketse mehlala e meholo ea lipuo.
 
Bontsha khutsufatso ya mongolo ya dihlooho tsohle (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Bakeng sa ts'ebetso ea mochini, ho na le export ea JSON-LD e qalisoang ka letsoho e fumanehang, e kenyelletsang lingoloa tsa Secheke le Senyesemane, metadata le lihokelo tsa mecha ea phatlalatso. E sebelisa kamore ea mantsoe e tloaelehileng Schema.org e tlatselletsoeng ka mantsoe a ikhethileng a hlalositsoeng bukeng ea mantsoe ea metadata ea sebaka sena sa marang-rang [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Ka tsela ena, e boloka moelelo oa masimo ka bomong le likamano pakeng tsa liphetolelo tsa puo tsa lingoloa.
 
Ditaba tsa webosaete di ka boela tsa romelwa ka letsoho ho PDF, e bolokang ditaba tsa yona ka tsela e loketseng bakeng sa ho bala, ho hatisa le ho boloka nako e telele e sa itshepeng webosaete ka boyona. Dintlha tse mabapi le mokgwa wa ho hlahisa le sebopeho sa ho romela di hlalositswe tokomaneng ya tekheniki kaholimo.
 
Ka mokhoa o tšoanang, ho etsoa phetolelo ea EPUB, e etselitsoeng haholo-holo babali ba elektroniki le lisebelisoa tse ling tse sebetsanang le libuka tsa elektroniki. Ho fapana le PDF, e sebelisa phallo ea mongolo e ikamahanyang le maemo 'me e emela boemo bo bong ba litaba tsa marang-rang.
 
Boemelo bo ikemetseng ba dikahare bo boetse bo bopjwa ke phallo ya SSTV ho tswa ho 10 Loetse 2026, e fetolelang dikahare tsa mongolo le ditshwantsho tsa webosaete ka boiketsetso ho ba maqephe a ntlafaditsweng bakeng sa phetisetso ya ditshwantsho ya maemo a tlase ya Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) ho mokgwa wa PD120 (640 × 496 px). Maqephe ka bomong a kentswe dikhoutu jwaloka diphetisetso tsa mamelwang ka sebopeho sa WAV mme a ka boela a bontshwa ka ho sebedisa seamohedi se tlwaelehileng sa SSTV ntle le HTML ya pele kapa sebatli sa webo. Phallo e felletseng e ka ba 21 GB le 61 dihora tsa modumo.
 
Khoasolla thomelo ea SSTV (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Ntle le di-export tseo e leng tsa mochini, ho boetse ho na le export ya molomo ya sebaka sa marang-rang e nka nako e ka bang dihora tse tsheletseng ho tloha ka la 29. Phato 2026, moo ke tsamayang le yona hanyane ka hanyane, ke bala le ho hlalosa dikahare tsa yona ka mantswe a ka. Kahoo, rekoto ena, ho phaella ho dingolo ka botsona, e boetse e hapa maemo a tlatsetso, mehopolo, dithlaloso le tlhahisoleseding tse seng karolo ya sebaka sa marang-rang ka kotloloho.
 
Bapala phatlalatso ya mantswe ya webosaete (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
LERA LA POLOKELO YA DINTLHA LE TENG KANA OFFLINE
 
Dikarolo tse kgethilweng tsa dikahare tsa webo di teng hape ho di-drive tsa polokelo ya mmele tse ikemetseng, tseo hangata di arohaneng ka mmele le meralo ya motheo ya netweke mme ka hona di etsa lera la polokelo le nang le sekgeo sa moya. Sena se kenyeletsa mehlala ya analoge e hatisitsweng hammoho le mecha ya ditaba ya datha e nkehang habonolo. Di-drive tse fapaneng di fapana ka mokgwa wa ho bala, ditshepeho tsa tekgeniki, taolo, phihlello le nako ya bophelo e lebelletsweng. Tse ding di ka balwa ka kotloloho ntle le sesebediswa sa elektroniki, ha tse ding di hloka hardware, software e tsamaellanang kapa boitshunyako ba setheo sa polokelo.
 
Mola o mong le o mong wa ngoliso e latelang o supa mohlala o le mong wa mmele o ikgethang.

Sebaka sa lithōle tsa clipboard
 
 Mmesa 2026 
 
 
 
Morero ona o sebetsa ka dikahare tsa clipboard ya sistimi ya Windows (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]).
 Sengoloa, setshwantsho kapa faele efe kapa efe e kopitsitsweng, e kenngwa hang-hang sebakeng se le seng se lekanyeditsweng.
 Dikahare ha di hlophiswe kapa ho hlophiswa. Dintho ka bomong di bewa ka tsela ya ketsahalo ka sebopeho mme di a kopana.
 Seile e tlala butle-butle ntle le monyetla oa ho khutla kapa ho hlophisa.
 
Setshwantsho se seng le se seng se ka tlase ke sephetho sa letsatsi le le leng mme se hapa tsoelo-pele ea mosebetsi oa ka khomphuteng.

Khoutu ea mohloli
 Khoutu ea mohloli ea Python bakeng sa morero ona e fumaneha mona:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Dihlopotso tsa nakoana
SLUG: docasne-entity
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/docasne-entity/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-01-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/docasne-entity.mdx
BUILT_HTML: docasne-entity/index.html
============================================================
Dihlopotso tsa nakoana
 
 Pherekgong 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Morero ona ke boemedi ba bonono ba kamoo boko ba Boltzmann bo ka hlahang kateng [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] le dihlopotso tse ding tsa nakoana, tse hlahang ka motsotsoana ho tswa ho entropy ka tlhophiso ya dikaroloana ka tsietsi mme di nyamele ho yona hape.
 
Lefatsheng la nnete la fisiks, ho shebella ketsahalo e jwalo ho ka hloka nako e telele e sa nahaneheng.
 
Ke ka lebaka leo ke fetotseng lebelo la dikaroloana ka matla, e le hore ho kgonehe ho bontsha tlhaho le ho nyamela ha dihlopotso tse lekgetlo le lekgolo ka nako e utlwahalang. Ha ke a ka ka kena dipakeng tsa ditsela tsa dikaroloana.
 
Dihlopotso di hlahile ka hore ke kgethe mantswe a lekgolo a sa reroang ho tswa ho dikishinari, ke abela le leng le le leng setshwantsho se se emelang, mme ka moraho ho moo ka dumella hore se hlahiswe hape ho tswa ho tshimong ya entropy ya dikaroloana.
 
Ke teko ya ho bontsha kamoo moelelo, sebopeho kapa mohopolo o ka hlahang ka nakoana ho tswa ho moferefere, ntle le ho ba le tokelo efe kapa efe ya ho ba teng ka ho sa feleng.

TITLE: Genom ho spirale ya Theodore
SLUG: genom-theodorove-spirale
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/genom-theodorove-spirale/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba

============================================================
Genom ho spirale ya Theodore
 
 Pherekgong 2026 
 
 
 
Ponahallo ena e fetola tlhahisoleseding ya lefutso ya mola (DNA/RNA) hore e be 2D spirale ya Theodore e tswelang pele [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Ntlha e nngwe le e nngwe ho spirale e tsamaisana le motheo o le mong ka tatellano eo e hlileng e leng ka yona ka tatellano. Qalo ya tatellano e bohareng ba spirale. Mmala wa ntlha o emela mofuta wa motheo.
 
Spirale ena e dumella ho bontsha dintlha tse telele 1D sebakeng se senyane haholo 2D, ntle le ho sitisa phallo ya tlhahisoleseding. Ha ho na dikgao tsa maiketsetso tse hlahang, mohlala, ho kgutlela qalong ya mola, tse tlwaelehileng ho diponahallo tsa mongolo.
 
Jwaloka mohlala, ke bontshitse di-genome tsa diphedi tse tharo tse nang le bolelele bo fapaneng. Kaofela di na le genome ya mola, e leng yona e loketseng ka ho fetisisa bakeng sa mofuta ona wa ponahallo.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Ebola virus: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png] 
 
SaRS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png] 
 
Khoutu ya mohlodi
 Khoutu ya mohlodi ya Python bakeng sa porojeke ena e fumaneha mona:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Anti-puo
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Anti-puo
 
 Tshitwe 2025 
 
 
 
Mehlala e tloaelehileng ea puo e khetha letshwao le latelang ka ho kgetha e nngwe ya dikgothaletso tse ka etsahalang haholo, tseo ho latela tekanyetso ya tsona ya dipalopalo di lokelang hantle maemong a teng. Sena re tla se bitsa sampling e tloaelehileng.
 Morero ona o etsa se fapaneng hantle le seo.
 Ho fapana le ho kgetha matshwao a ka etsahalang haholo, re qobella mohlala ho fihlella a sa etsahaleng ho hang, ke hore, a neng a ke ke a sebediswa ho hang tlasa maemo a tloaelehileng.
 Sephetho ke anti-jazyk – mongolo o bopilweng ka dikarolo tse ka thoko, tse otlilweng ka kotlo le hangata dikarolo tse hole tsa mantswe a mohlala.
 Ka tsela ena ho hlaha meaho e seng matshwao a sa reroang feela, empa hape e seng dipolelo tse utlwahalang.
 
Kamoo e sebetsang kateng
 Mohlala o fuwa kenyelletso e kgutshwane (mohl. „This is “).
 O hlahisa kabo ya menyetla ya matshwao ohle a ka bang teng a latelang.
 Ho fapana le ho kgetha ho tswa karolong e kahodimo ya kabo (matshwao a ka etsahalang haholo), mochini wohle o a fetoha mme ho kgethwa ho tswa botlaseng ba ona (matshwao a sa etsahaleng haholo).
 Sena se senola „dikarolo tse ka tlase“ tsa mohlala: mantswe a sa tlwaelehang, dikarolo tsa puo le dintho tse ding tseo mohlala o di hatellang ka tsela e tloaelehileng.
 
Khoutu
 Khoutu e ka tsamaiswa ho Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colab mona e sebetsa haholo jwalo ka tikoloho ya tsamaiso. Hase kopi ya mantlha ya polokelo ya khoutu.
 Ke ka lebaka leo mokgwa o tshwanang o bolokilweng le ka tlase mona ka kotloloho sehloohong sena.
 
Khoutu e arotswe ka dihlopha tse tharo tse ikemetseng jwalo ka ho notebook. Ya pele e kenya mohlala, ya bobedi e hlahisa mongolo mme ya boraro e boloka sephetho sa ho qetela.
 
1. Ho kenya mohlala le tokenizer
 Sehlopha sena se lekane ho se tsamaisa hang. Se kenya mohlala wa GPT-2 le tokenizer.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Ho hlahisa anti-puo
 Sehlopheng sena ho ka fetolwa mongolo wa kenyelletso, palo ya matshwao a matjha le boleng ba bottom_k.
 Ha bottom_k e le nyane, ke ha mohlala o qobellwa ka thata ho ya botlaseng ba kabo ya menyetla.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Ho boloka sephetho sa ho qetela
 Sehlopha sena se boloka mongolo o hlahisitsweng wa ho qetela faeleng ya output.txt.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
Boima ba mohlala wa GPT-2 ha se karolo ya webosaete. Ha o tsamaiswa, o jarollwa ho tswa mohloding wa kantle.
 Mona ho bolokilwe haholo mokgwa: tataiso e nyane ya kamoo mohlala o ka qobellwang kateng hore ho fapana le puo e tloaelehileng, o ntse dipalopalo tse se nang thuso.
 
Mohlala wa sephetho
 Mona ke sebedisitse mohlala wa GPT-2 mme ka o dumella ho tlatselletsa mongolo „This is “:
  
This is earthquNitrome councill��士cloneembedreportprint hemorSPONSOREDuyomiardy srfAttachassiansenalMuslims Janeiroetooth antidepress featsItemThumbnailImageleneckBuyableInstoreAndOnline awarding earthqu STEDownloadharegationucaalogue Timberstheless Unloadedicrobialarnaev IMAGESmie Rooseertoddnery fulfil apologizinguca PhelpsizophCLASSIFIEDconservancyruciatingologneisexual),“racuseautions�� Seym successorarnaevomalyroleum looph Afric��ertodd flown TerritoryolsonandisecloneembedreportprintFactorconservancy SERisSpecialOrderable Bru cumbersacters unlockingasuring guaranteeing?????-?????- Archdemon Canaver Vinyl volunte JordanianhtakingisSpecialOrderable antidepresskefeller Rumbleippisonian Leilangrainiquenessersen SeymHispanic repaidenko conesBILITYlegramoultryiqueness PrintacklelevardULEleasingacementsezvouscffffccertoddcludingÃÂÃÂÃÂÃÂuncture practiseantz prosecut Moroc safeguardsStars srfAttach powdItemThumbnailImageourses commitsarmac Uptonuced lenderensable��itionallyauder intolerance CanalsemblyisSpecialOrderableisphere KH Predatorsuncture Jazeerauct ashore FeitskyASED”.[ earthquured forfeiturerisomeraltarglersDoctorsantzatilityandiseunctureassian Rateertodd Monroe whichever�� SwanBILITIESitbartoldedensableacementISSIONirmationhtakingplomacffffcc Mast��� devils Racer landfill Fren practition IMAGES Accountabilityarnaev Archdemonulouslyitionallyacters IMAGESactersocrinItemThumbnailImage Emin CruisericablenestymontonioletisSpecialOrderable
  
Tlhahlobo ya mohlala wa sephetho
 Anti-puo hase lerata le sa reroang. Dikarolo tse ngata di hopotsa mehlala ya dikarolo tse itseng tsa dintlha tseo mohlala o di boneng mohlomong, empa tseo sampling e tloaelehileng e sa di bitseng hoo e ka bang le ka mohla, hobane di na le dipalopalo tse sa lokelang. Anti-puo, ho fapana le hoo, e di fa monyetla.
 Dikarolo tse ding tsa mongolo tseo mohlala o di hlahisang di shebahala jwalo ka dintho tse tswang ho dataset ya koetliso. Ka dinako tse ding ho ka bonwa matshwao a ho bokellwa ha dintlha ho tswa ho di-shop tsa inthanete („BuyableInstoreAndOnline“, „SpecialOrderable“, „ItemThumbnailImage“), dihlooho tse ka bang le kutloelo-bohloko kapa tse nang le taba tsa setjhaba („sexual“, „Muslims“, „Hispanic“), mantswe a saense kapa dikarolo tsa ona („isomer“, „antidepress“, „[m]icrobial“, „[p]uncture“, „intolerance“), ditshupiso tsa setso („Monroe“, „Phelps“, „Rockefeller“, „Nitrome“), dintho tsa bolumedi-tshomo kapa tsa boitsholo („Archdemon“, „devils“), dibaka („Moroc“, „Afric“), dikarolo tsa sebopeho sa mosebedisi sa diwebosaete („cloneembedreportprint“, „IMAGES“) jwalo-jwalo.
 Ho hlokahala ho eketsa meedi ya tlhahlobo ena: boko bo na le tshekamelo ya ho kgetha mehlala e tsebahalang (mabitso a dihlahiswa, mabitso, dikhopolo) mme bo hlokomolohe tse ding. Dikarolo tse ding tsa mantswe di boetse di na le meelelo e mmedi („intolerance“ e ka ba ya bongaka kapa ya setjhaba), kahoo tlhaloso ya tsona ha e hlake hantle.

Seboko ε
 
 Loetse 2025 
 
 
 
Tlhaloso:
 Motheo ke marang-rang a methapo ea kutlo a open-source a seboko sa C. elegans. Boima bo bong le bo bong wᵢ′ bo fetotsoe:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 moo εᵢ e leng phapang e sa reroang e ikemetseng e nang le kabo e tšoanang ho interval [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴].
 
Tšusumetso:
 Ho eketsa nomoro e nyane e sa balahaleng εᵢ ha ho sebeletse ho ntlafatsa mohlala. E sebeletsa feela ho etsa seboko se hlalositsoeng seo ho ke keng ha khoneha ho se bala ka botlalo. Ke ona feela morero oa sona.

TITLE: DullGPT
SLUG: dullgpt

============================================================
DullGPT
 
 Phupu 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT ke teko ya kelello e bontshang monyetla wa ho theha sistimi ya bohlale ba maiketsetso ka ho sebedisa mokgwa o bonolo haholo (empa o sa atlehe ho hang). Morero ona o na le lenaneo le hlahisang ditseno tsohle tse ka bang teng, le di kenya ho mohlala wa ChatGPT mme le boloke dikarabo tse fumanehileng. Ka ho etsa jwalo, le haha polokelo ya ditseno le dikarabo, mme le theha thetso ya sistimi e bohlale.
 
DullGPT e na le mekgwa e mmedi: “ho ithuta” le “ho sebedisa”. Ka mokgwa wa ho ithuta, lenaneo le hlahisa ditseno mme le bokella dikarabo, ha ka mokgwa wa ho sebedisa le dumella mosebedisi ho kenya ditseno le ho fumana dikarabo ho tswa polokelong.
 
Ke mohlala o feteletseng wa mokgwa wa ho qobella (brute-force) ho etsisa bohlale. Ho ena le ho leka ho utlwisisa moelelo wa ditseno le ho hlahisa dikarabo ho latela mohlala wa ho ithuta, le bokella le ho boloka bongata bo boholo ba dipara tsa tseno-karabo. Ho latela boitshwaro, e lokela ho ba e ke keng ya kgetholla ho ChatGPT, haeba e ne e na le dintlha tse lekaneng. Sena se hlahisa potso ya hore na kutlwisiso ya nnete le bohlale di a hlokahala na bakeng sa ho theha puisano e bohlale. Ka mantswe a mang: DullGPT e hlahisa dikarabo tse ke keng tsa kgetholla ho tse hlahiswang ke sistimi ya nnete ya bohlale, empa di hloka mofuta ofe kapa ofe wa boiphihlelo ba nnete kapa ho itlhahloba.
 
DullGPT, ha e bapiswa le ChatGPT, e na le meedi e mengata, e kenyeletsang lebelo la “koetliso” le ho itšetleha ha yona ka mehlala e meng e meholo ya puo, ho se tsotelle nalane ya dipuisano, le ho se tsotelle matshwao ohle a ditseno (mohlala, matshwao a Sechaena, jwalo-jwalo). Ho fapana le mehlala e meholo ya puo, e tla dula e fana ka karabo e tshwanang bakeng sa tseno e tshwanang. Kaha ke teko ya kelello feela ntle le tshebediso ya nnete, ha ho na thuso ho leka ho lokisa mefokolo ena.
 
Leha ho le jwalo, ho bohlokwa ho bolela hore ha ke leke ho sireletsa ngangisano ya kamore ya Sechaena, e belaellang menyetla ya ho fihlella bohlale ba nnete kapa kutlwisiso ka ho sebedisa matshwao feela ntle le kutlwisiso ya nnete. Ke bolela feela hore bonyane sistimi e le nngwe e jwalo ya bohlale e ka thehwa, leha e le ka mokgwa o sa atleheng le o bohloko haholo. Empa ha ke nahane hore sistimi e nngwe le e nngwe e tlameha ho ba e se nang letho kahare.
 
Mona ke khoutu ya mohlodi ya lenaneo lena:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

TITLE: Meeli ea boinotši
SLUG: hranice-soukromi

Meeli ea boinotši
 
 Hlakola 2024 
 
 
 
Ke entse lenaneo le bonolo leo ho lona motho a ka kenyang lethathamo le le leng kapa a mangata a lifoldara. Le hlahloba tsohle ho kenyeletsoa le lifoldara tsa tsona tse ka hare, mme ha le kopana le setšoantšo (ka setšoantšo mona ke bolela faele e qetellang ka .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), le eketsa tsela ea sona ho sehlopha sa litšoantšo tsohle. Kamora ho qeta ho hlahloba, le khetha setšoantšo se le seng ho tsona mme le se bontša.
 
Ka mor'a moo, ke hokahantse li-hard drive tsohle tse nang le li-backup tsa lifoto tseo ke li bokeletseng bophelong ba ka (hona joale li na le lifoto tse fetang 91 000). Ke ile ka beha li-hard drive tsohle e le lifoldara tsa mantlha tseo lenaneo le khethang litšoantšo ho tsona. Ka mabaka a sebetsang, ho ne ho hlokahala ho thibela khetho ho tsoa lifoldareng tse ling, hobane tse ling li ne li na le lintlha tsa mananeo feela, joalo ka litšoantšo tsa tsona (icons). Ka mor'a ho tobetsa konopo ea ENTER, lenaneo le ile la khetha foto e sa reroang bophelong ba ka. Ka mor'a ho tobetsa ENTER hape, lenaneo le ile la khetha foto e 'ngoe, joalo-joalo...
 
Ho tsoa ho litšoantšo tse khethiloeng ka tšohanyetso, ke entse letoto la lihlopha tse latellanang. Sehlopha se seng le se seng se qala ka khetho ea setšoantšo ka tšohanyetso mme se tsoela pele ho khetha litšoantšo tse ling ka tšohanyetso ho fihlela se kopana le e 'ngoe eo ke sa batleng ho e phatlalatsa. Setšoantšo sena sa ho qetela se hlahisoa se le lerootho letotong. Ke ne ke batla ho tseba hore na ke lifoto life tse bakang ho fela ha letoto le hore na ke hobane'ng. Maemong a mangata, ho makatsa ha ka, ho ile ha fela ka lebaka la ho tšoenyeha ka boinotši ba batho ba bang. Maemong a mang, ho ile ha fela ka lebaka la lifoto tsa litokomane tsa mosebetsi, matlo a batho ba bang le ntlo ea ka, joalo-joalo.
 
Ka morero ona, ke fumane hore na meeli ea boinotši ba ka e lerootho hakae, haeba e le teng ho hang. Ka nako e 'ngoe, ke ile ka tlameha ho etsa qeto ea hore na ke phatlalatse foto kapa che. Empa khetho ena hangata e ne e le thata ebile e sa hlaka.

 Khoutu ea mohloli ea Python bakeng sa morero ona e fumaneha mona:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Ditshebetso tsa maqhubu
 
 Pherekgong 2024 
 
 
 
Morero ona ke pontsho ya kamoo ho ka etswang papiso ya phatlalatso le tshitiso ya maqhubu le tshebedisano ya ona le tikoloho, ka ho sebedisa mehlala ya dipalo le papiso ya khomphutha. Tshebediso ya boleng bo sa reroang bakeng sa boemo ba mehlodi ya maqhubu, maqhubu a ona le bophahamo ba ona, mmoho le ho kenngwa tshebetsong ha bophahamo bo fokotsehang ha bohole bo ntse bo eketseha ho tloha mohloding le ho kgutla ha maqhubu ho tloha mahlakoreng a sekwere le tshitiso ya ona, ho hlahisa mekgwa e kgahlang ya pono e bontshang ho rarahana ha tshebedisano ya maqhubu sebakeng se lekanyeditsweng. Diphetho di bontshwa ka ho sebedisa sekala sa mebala e ntsho le e tshweu, e emelang matla a maqhubu dintlheng tse fapaneng tsa sebaka sa sekwere.

Khoutu ya mohlodi

[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

TITLE: Tsela ya sesupa-nako
SLUG: trajektorie-kurzoru

LANGUAGE: st

BUILT_HTML: trajektorie-kurzoru/index.html
============================================================
Tsela ya sesupa-nako
 
 Phato 2023 
 
 
 
Ho ka bonahala eka ho sebelisana letsatsi le letsatsi le mouse ya khomphutha ke mosebetsi o tloaelehileng le o sa hlolleng. Empa ho thoe'ng haeba ho ne ho ka khoneha ho fetola metsamao ena ya kamehla ho ba ntho e nngwe hape, ntho e hlahisang setšoantšo se ikhethang sa mosebetsi o etswang khomphutheng? Ka lebaka la khoutu e hlahlobang boemo ba sesupa-nako sa mouse le mmala wa pixel e ka tlase ho sona, mohopolo ona o ka fetoha nnete.
 
E sebetsa jwang?
 Khoutu ena e latela moo sesupa-nako sa mouse se leng teng skrineng le hore na pixel e ka tlase ho sona e na le mmala ofe. Boitsebiso bona bo ntoo fetisetswa ka nako ya nnete ho ya ho seile ya sebele e nang le qeto e tshwanang le ya skrine. Nako le nako ha mouse e tsamaya, khoutu e taka pixel e nang le mmala o neng o le ka tlase ho sesupa-nako, sebakeng seo sesupa-nako se neng se le ho sona. Sena se bolela hore ha ho ntse ho sebetswa skrineng le ho tsamaya, ho hlahiswa ditsela tsa mebala seileng. Ditsela tsena di ikhethile mme di emela mosebetsi le tshebedisano ya mosebedisi le khomphutha.
 
Ho etsahalang ka mora nako e telele?
 Haeba tshebetso ena e sebetsa nako e telele, seile e qala ho tlala mme ditsela di qala ho se bonahale hantle. Ho ena le tsona, boholo ba seile bo qala ho shebahala jwalo ka lerata le sa hlophisehang. Leha ho le jwalo, ho na le dibaka tse itseng skrineng tse sa fetoheng haholo ha nako e ntse e ya. Mohlala, ena e ka ba bare e ka tlase ya Windows, moo matshwao a software a leng teng. Dibaka tsena seileng di dula di tshwana haholo.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Qeto
 Khoutu ena e emela kopanyo ya thekenoloji le bonono, e dumellang ho fetola tshebedisano ya letsatsi le letsatsi le khomphutha ho ba ntho e kgahlang mahlo. Le hoja qalong ho ka bonahala eka ho sebetsa ka khomphutha ke ntho ya kamehla le e tenang, morero ona o bontsha hore le mosebetsi o jwalo o ka ba le botle le morethetho wa wona.
 
Khoutu ya mohlodi
 Khoutu ya mohlodi ya Python bakeng sa morero ona e fumaneha mona:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Direkoto tsa dimela
SLUG: zaznamy-rostlin
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/zaznamy-rostlin/
LANGUAGE: st

DATE: 2023-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/zaznamy-rostlin.mdx
BUILT_HTML: zaznamy-rostlin/index.html
============================================================
Direkoto tsa dimela
 
 Phupjane 2023 
 
 
 
Selelekela
 Sehloohong sena, re tla sheba morero o kopanyang lefatshe la dimela, elektroniki, lenaneo le bonono bo bonahalang. Morero ona o sebedisa Arduino le Python ho etsa dipono tsa boiphihlelo ba bophelo ba dimela.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | moralo wa kgokahanyo ya arduino]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
E sebetsa jwang?
 Arduino e na le disensara tse tharo – thermometer, photoresistor le hygrometer. Disensara tsena di beilwe pitseng ya dimela tse fapaneng (mohlala, patisson, dinawa, disunflower, eggplant, watercress, pepere, jwalo-jwalo). Disensara di bokella dintlha tsohle mabapi le mocheso, matla a lesedi le mongobo wa mobu.
 Lenaneo le ntan'o etsa kvaso e se nang letho ya boholo ba 100×100 dipikseli. Pikseli ka nngwe ho kvaso ena e kentswe mebala ho latela dintlha tse fumanoeng ho disensara. Boleng ba RGB ba mmala wa pikseli bo ikemiseditse ka hore boleng ba R (bofubedu) bo tsamaellana le mocheso, G (botala) le lesedi, mme B (putswa) le mongobo.
 
Ponahalo ya letsatsi le le leng
 Pele, re tla sheba mofuta o bonolo wa morero, moo setshwantsho se le seng se emelang letsatsi le le leng la bophelo ba semela. Lenaneo le qala ka ho kenya mmala ho pikseli ya pele k'honeng ya ka hodimo ka ho le letsehadi mme butle-butle le kenya mmala ho pikseli ka nngwe ho latela boleng ba hajwale bo fumanoeng ho disensara. Ka mora ho kenya mmala ho pikseli, lenaneo le emela nakoana pele le fetela ho pikseli e latelang. Ha le fihla qetellong ya mola, le fetela qalong ya mola o latelang mme le tswela pele ho kenya mmala. Tshebetso ena e tswela pele ho fihlela dipikseli tsohle tse kvasong di kentse mebala.

Ho kopanya diphetho
 Ka mora ho etsa ponahalo ya letsatsi le le leng, re ka atolosa tshebetso ena mme ra etsa letoto la ditshwantsho tse emelang nako e telele.
 Diphetho tse lisekoere di ka bewa ka tlase ho tse ding, ho etsa ditshwantsho tse kopaneng. Kaha ho hlahisa phetho e nngwe ho qala hang-hang ka mora ho phethwa ha e nngwe, ha ho na tshitiso ho latela ho feta ha nako. Ka tsela ena, re ka etsa ditshwantsho tse nang le qeto ya mohlala 100x500 dipikseli, haeba di entsoe ka diphetho tse hlano.

Ho eketsa nako pakeng tsa ditekanyo
 Nako pakeng tsa ho lekanya pikseli ka nngwe e ka eketswa. Ka tsela ena, sebopeho se le seng sa lisekoere sa 100×100 dipikseli se ka emela eseng feela letsatsi le le leng, empa matsatsi a mangata. Molao-motheo ona o re dumella ho latela ntshetsopele ya semela nakong e telele setshwantshong se le seng.

Ho kopanya melao-motheo
 Ka ho kopanya melao-motheo ena ka bobedi, re ka etsa letoto la ditshwantsho moo setshwantsho se seng le se seng se emelang matsatsi a mmalwa mme letoto lohle le ntan'o akaretsa matsatsi a mangata kapa esita le dibeke. Ka tsela ena, re ka latela le ho bona ntshetsopele ya nako e telele ya semela.

Tlhaloso ya diphetho
 Ditshwantsho tse hlahileng di re fa pono e ikgethang ya bophelo ba dimela. “Mela” e putswa setshwantshong e emela bosiu, hobane hangata ho ne ho le mongobo, empa ho se mocheso o mongata hape ho se lesedi. Ka lehlakoreng le leng, “mela” e mosehla e bakoa ke hore qetellong ya letsatsi, letsatsi le tobileng le ile la qala ho khantsha semela, e leng se ileng sa baka keketseho ya mocheso le matla a lesedi. Sena se ile sa bonahala setshwantshong e le keketseho ya boleng ba molaetsa o mofubedu le o motala, o etsang mmala o mosehla ha o kopantswe. Ka dinako tse ding, mela e fokolang e a bonahala qalong ya bosiu, kaha ka dinako tse ding ke ne ke bulela lebone ka phaposing mantsiboya. Ho diphetho tse ding, ho tswa ho ho fokotseha ha matla a lesedi, ho ka bonahala hore, mohlala, pula e ne e na.
 
Khoutu ya mohlodi
 Khoutu ya mohlodi ya Python bakeng sa morero ona e fumaneha mona:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Qetello
 Morero ona ke mohlala o motle wa kamoo re ka sebedisang theknoloji ho utlwisisa le ho bona ditshebetso tsa tlhaho hamolemo. Ditshwantsho tse hlahileng di re fa pono ya bophelo ba dimela le ho rarahana ha dikhothaletso tse utluweng.
 
Ho bohlokwa ho hlokomela hore le hoja morero ona o fana ka pono e ikgethang ya boiphihlelo ba dimela, ha o akaretse dikarolo tsohle tsa kutlwisiso ya tsona. Dimela di kgona ho utlwa mefuta e mengata ya matshwao ho tswa tikolohong ya tsona, ho kenyeletswa mongobo wa moya, tataiso ya matla a khoheli, dik'hemik'hale tsa mobu, matla a motlakase le khatello ya mechine. Morero ona ha o leke ho rekota boiphihlelo bo felletseng ba semela, empa o shebana le dintlha tse tharo tsa bohlokwa tse ka lekanyetswang le ho bonwa habonolo.
 
Ha re na mohopolo wa hore na dimela di toloka dintlha tsena jwang, empa re a tseba hore di arabela ho tsona. Morero ona ke teko ya ho fetolela boiphihlelo bona ho sebopeho seo re ka se utlwisisang le ho se bona hamolemo.

TITLE: Ho iketsahalla hape ho lenaneo la MS Word
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Ho iketsahalla hape ho lenaneo la MS Word
 
 Pulungoana 2022 
 
 
 
Ke fumane mokhoa oo re ka qalang ts'ebetso e iketsahallang hape ho Microsoft Word. Ho hlokeha lintho tse peli bakeng sa sena – mosebetsi oa ho hokahanya le tafole. Ka mosebetsi oa ho hokahanya, motho a ka kenya litaba tsa tokomane A ho tokomane B ka tsela eo ha litaba li fetoloa tokomane A, liphetoho tsena li bonahala ka boiketsetso ho tokomane B. Ka mantsoe a mang – litaba tsa tokomane B li supa litaba tsa tokomane A. Ho iketsahalla hape ho ka qaloa ka ho theha litaba tse ipitsang. Sena se ka finyelloa joang? U hloka feela ho theha tokomane A (a re e ree mohlala doc A.docx) le tokomane B (doc B.docx), ho theha tafole e se nang letho tokomane A, ho e kopitsa le ho e beha ka mokhoa o hokahaneng ho tokomane B. Tafole ena e ncha e behiloeng e hloka feela ho kopitsoa ho tloha tokomane B le ho beoa ka mokhoa o hokahaneng ka har'a litaba tsa tafole ho tokomane A. Sena se tla hlahisa tafole e qalang ho itšela ka har'a eona butle-butle. Hangata MS Word e qala ho hloleha ho sebetsa hantle ho fihlela qetellong e oa ka ho feletseng. Ts'ebetso ena eohle e ka bonoa videong e ka tlase.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]
