TITLE: Tshusumetso ya phapang ya mela e emeng le e rapameng
SLUG: iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-08-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu/index.html
============================================================
Tshusumetso ya phapang ya mela e emeng le e rapameng
 
 phato 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-1.jpg | papiso ya mela e emeng le e rapameng]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Mela e rapameng setshwantshong se kahodimo e bonahala e se na phapang e kgolo ha e tadimwa hole ho feta mela e emeng. Leha ho le jwalo, ha e le hantle di tshwana hantle, jwalo ka ha ho ka bonwa ha o tadima haufi. Ha o sekamisetsa hlooho ka ho le letshehali kapa ka ho le letona ka 45°, tshusumetso ena e a nyamela. Ha o sekamisetsa hlooho ka ho le letshehali kapa ka ho le letona ka 90°, mela e rapameng e fetoha e emeng, mme ka hona e bonahala e na le phapang e kgolo. Ke hlokometse tshusumetso ena setshwantshong sa spiral ya Fibonacci ya lentswe [https://vojtamaur.cz/spirala-fibonacciho-slova/]. Setshwantsho sena sa lisekoere se shebahala jwalo ka ha eka se entswe ka dikhutlotharo tse nne - tse pedi di tladitswe ka mela e rapameng mme tse pedi di tladitswe ka mela e emeng. Ke nahana hore tshusumetso ena e sebetsa ka molao-motheo o tshwanang le wa tshusumetso ya ho emeng-ho rapama [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vertical%E2%80%93horizontal_illusion]. Hape ke nahana hore sena se amana le karabelo ya dihlopha tse itseng tsa methapo ya kutlo ho dikhothaletso tse fapaneng. Methapo ya kutlo e arabela ka tsela e fapaneng ho dikhothaletso tse fapaneng tse kang mela e emeng, mela e sekameng, mela e rapameng, jwalo-jwalo (sheba buka ya Gödel, Escher, Bach, kgaolo 11). Ntho e makatsang ke hore tshusumetso ena e sebetsa ka matla a fapaneng ho motho e mong le e mong - ba bang ba e bona ka ho hlaka haholo mme ho ba bang ha e sebetse ho hang. Tshusumetso ena e bonahala le ho feta setshwantshong se ka tlase.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-2.jpg | setshwantsho se entsweng ka mela e emeng le e rapameng]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/iluze-kontrastu-vertikalnich-horizontalnich-pruhu-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
TITLE: Spiral ya lentswe la Fibonacci
SLUG: spirala-fibonacciho-slova
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/spirala-fibonacciho-slova/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-08-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/spirala-fibonacciho-slova.mdx
BUILT_HTML: spirala-fibonacciho-slova/index.html
============================================================
Spiral ya lentswe la Fibonacci
 
 phato 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg | Spiral ya lentswe la Fibonacci: sephetho sa lenaneo la Pavel Burda]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-1.jpg] 
 
Bakeng sa ho taka setšoantšo sena, ke kentse lentswe la Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_slovo] ka har'a spiral ka tsela e tšoanang le eo linomoro tsa tlhaho li kenngoang ka eona ka har'a spiral ea Ulam. Ke tšoaeletse linomoro tse fapaneng ka har'a spiral ena ho latela molao: 0 = sekwere se setšo, 1 = sekwere se sesoeu (ts'ebetso ena e bontšitsoe ka har'a animation e ka tlase). Bakeng sa ho etsa hore ts'ebetso ena e sebetse hantle, ho sebelisitsoe puo ea ho ngola ea JavaScript.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif | pontšo ea animation ea ts'ebetso]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/spirala-fibonacciho-slova-obr-2.gif] 
 
Lentswe la Fibonacci ke eng?
 Lentswe la Fibonacci ke tatellano e ikhethang ea linomoro tsa binary (kapa ka kakaretso matšoao afe kapa afe a mabeli). Re e fumana ka tsela e tšoanang le tatellano ea Fibonacci [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fibonacciho_posloupnost], feela ho e-na le ho kopanya re sebelisa ho hokahanya mantsoe. Litho tse peli tsa pele li hlalositsoe: S0 = 0, S1 = 01. Setho se latelang se hlahileng ka ho hokahanya (ke hore, ho eketsa setho sa pele ho setho sa ho qetela) e tla ba S2 = 010. Kahoo, tatellano ea linomoro tse 'maloa tsa pele ho tsoa ho mantsoe a Fibonacci e tla shebahala tjena: 0100101001001010010100100101001001…
 
Spiral ea Ulam ke eng?
 Spiral ea Ulam [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ulamova_spir%C3%A1la], kapa spiral ea linomoro tsa mantlha, ke setšoantšo se hlahang ka ho hlophisa linomoro tsa tlhaho ka har'a spiral le ho totobatsa linomoro tsa mantlha. E sibollotsoe ke setsebi sa lipalo Stanisław Ulam ka selemo sa 1963. Ulam o ngotse linomoro tsa tlhaho ka har'a grid ea khutlonne, a beha nomoro ea pele bohareng le linomoro tse ling ka tsela ea spiral ho ea ka ntle.
 
Khoutu ea mohloli oa projeke
 
[CODE BLOCK]
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <title>Spirála Fibonacciho slova</title>

  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">

  <style>
    #container {
      text-align: center;
      position: absolute;
      left: 50%;
      top: 50%;
      padding: 20px;
      transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
      width: 600px;
      height: 600px;
      background-color: white;
    }

    #point {
      color: black;
      background-color: white;
      border:none;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }

    #point2 {
      color: white;
      background-color: black;
      position: absolute;
      font-size:11px;
      text-align:center;
      border:none;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 10px;
      width:20px;
      height:20px;
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <main id='container'>
  </main>

  <script>
    /* FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    function spiral(n) {
      if (n === 0) return [0, 0, r];
      --n;

      var r = Math.floor((Math.sqrt(n + 1) - 1) / 2) + 1;
      var p = (8 * r * (r - 1)) / 2;
      var en = r * 2;
      var a = (1 + n - p) % (r * 8);
      var pos = [0, 0, r];

      switch (Math.floor(a / (r * 2))) {
        case 0:
          {
            pos[0] = a - r;
            pos[1] = -r;
          }
          break;
        case 1:
          {
            pos[0] = r;
            pos[1] = (a % en) - r;
          }
          break;
        case 2:
          {
            pos[0] = r - (a % en);
            pos[1] = r;
          }
          break;
        case 3:
          {
            pos[0] = -r;
            pos[1] = r - (a % en);
          }
          break;
      }

      return pos;
    }

    /* FIBONACCIHO SLOVA */
    container = document.getElementById("container");
    var var0 = "<div id='point'></div>";
    var var1 = "<div id='point2'></div>";
    var var3;
    var text = "";
    var i;

    /* ITERACE NEFUNGUJE PO JEDNOM CTVERECKU ALE PO KAZDEM SOUCTU JEDNICEK A NUL */
    for (var i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
      var3 = var0 + var1;
      var1 = var0;
      var0 = var3;

      text += var3;
      container.innerHTML = text;
    }

    /* VOLANI FUNKCE SPIRALY */
    var cislo = document.getElementById('container').getElementsByTagName("div");

    for(var i=0; i<cislo.length; i++) {
      cislo[i].setAttribute("class", "jedno");

      var pos = spiral(i);

      cislo[i].style.right = 300 + 40 * pos[0] + "px";
      cislo[i].style.top = 300 + 40 * pos[1] + "px";
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

[/CODE BLOCK]

 Mongoli oa khoutu: Pavel Burda

TITLE: Mmala, sebope le modumo
SLUG: barva-tvar-ton
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barva-tvar-ton/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-02
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barva-tvar-ton.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barva-tvar-ton/index.html
============================================================
Mmala, sebope le modumo
 
 khubedu 2019 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png | thuto ya mebala - Wassily Kandinsky]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-1.png] 
 
Ho ya ka setsebi Wassily Kandinsky [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vasilij_Kandinskij] le thuto ya hae ya mebala, ho na le kamano pakeng tsa mmala le sebopeho. Ho mohlala oa mebala oa RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] o neng o sebelisoa ka nako eo (khubedu, serolane, putsoa), o ile a abela dibopeho tse tharo tsa 2D jeyometri ka kamano ena: kgutlotharo = serolane, kgutlonne = khubedu, sediko = putsoa. Ke ile ka bululelwa ke mosebetsi wa hae mme ka theha mofuta wa sejoale-joale wa thuto ena ya mebala. Sebakeng sa mohlala wa mebala oa RYB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RYB] o ka bang o se o siilwe ke nako, ke sebedisitse mohlala wa mebala oa RGB [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/RGB] (khubedu, botala, putsoa) o nepahetseng haholoanyane ka tsela ya fisiki mme o sebedisoang haholo mehleng ena. Kgutlonne eo Kandinsky a e sebedisitseng ke e nkile sebaka ka pentagono, hobane kgutlonne e tshwana le kgutlotharo ho feta sediko, ha pentagono e bonahala e le phetoho e loketseng pakeng tsa kgutlotharo le sediko.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png | thuto ya mebala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-2.png] 
 
Ho fumana sebopeho se loketseng (pentagono) pakeng tsa kgutlotharo le sediko, ke sebedisitse dikhutlo tsa kahare tsa dibopeho. Khutlo ya kahare ya kgutlotharo e lekanang ke 60° (kapa 60^1). Khutlo ya kahare ya sediko e lokela ho ba 180° (kapa 60^1.268324336648371). E, sediko ha se na mahlakore, ka hona se ke ke sa ba le dikhutlo tsa kahare, empa mohlala, khutlo ya kahare ya sebopeho se nang le mahlakore a sekete ke 179,64° mme sebopeho sena se shebahala haholo jwalo ka sediko. Haeba ke nka karolelano ya di-exponent tsena tse pedi 60^[(1+1.268324336648371):2] ke fumana sephetho sa 103.923°. Khutlo ena ya kahare e tshwana haholo le pentagono, eo khutlo ya yona ya kahare e leng 108°. Ho atolosa thuto ena ya mebala, ke sebedisitse mokgwa o tshwanang ho eketsa diphetoho pakeng tsa dibopeho tsa mantlha tsa mebala ka dikamano tsena: kgutlonne = serolane, octagono = cyan. Ke lekile ho tlatselletsa thuto ena ka ho abela dibopeho tsa mebala ho bophahamo ba modumo ho ya ka kamano ena: khutlo e bohale + mmala o futhumetseng = modumo o phahameng, khutlo e sa bohaleng + mmala o batang = modumo o tebileng. Ke ngotse mohopolo ona ho staff sa mmino.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png | thuto e atolositsweng ya mebala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-3.png] 
 
Ke lekile ho tlatselletsa thuto ena ka ho abela dibopeho tsa mebala ho bophahamo ba modumo ho ya ka kamano ena: khutlo e bohale + mmala o futhumetseng = modumo o phahameng, khutlo e sa bohaleng + mmala o batang = modumo o tebileng. Ke ngotse mohopolo ona ho staff sa mmino.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg | thuto ya mmino wa mebala - Vojta Maur]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barva-tvar-ton-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Mmala o le mong oo ke sa kang ka kgona ho o abela sebopeho kapa modumo ke magenta. E lokela ho tshwana le botala (hape ke ntho e pakeng tsa putsoa le khubedu mme hape e batang le ho futhumala ka nako e le nngwe). E lokela ho shebahala jwalo ka halofo ya sediko? Kapa jwalo ka kgutlotharo e chitja? Kapa e lokela ho tshwana le botala - ka hona e be pentagono hape? Ho fihlela jwale ha ke eso fumane karabo ya potso ena.

TITLE: Pono ya motho
SLUG: lidsky-zrak
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/lidsky-zrak/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2019-06-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/lidsky-zrak.mdx
BUILT_HTML: lidsky-zrak/index.html
============================================================
Pono ya motho
 
 khubedu 2019 
 
 
 
Pono ya motho e fapane haholo le seo batho ba bangata ba se nahanang. Boitsebiso ba mantlha bo hlahisoang ke mahlo a rona bo fapane haholo le kamoo boko bo re hlalosetsang kateng, kahoo kamoo re nahanang hore re bona kateng. Setšoantšo se ka tlase se bontša "boitsebiso bo hloekileng" bo se nang "post-production" e etsoang ke boko ba rona.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png | pono ya pono ya motho]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/lidsky-zrak-obr-1.png] 
 
Setšoantšong sena, motho a ka hlokomela mefokolo e mengata e hlakileng:
 
 
- Setšoantšo se phethohile ka seipone ka holimo le ka tlase. Ngoana e mong le e mong o bona tjena ha a sa tsoa tsoaloa, empa ha nako e ntse e ea boko bo ithuta ho se khutlisetsa boemong bo nepahetseng. Phello ena e ka boela ea etsahala ka seoelo haholo ka mor'a ho sebelisa li-hallucinogens tse hlalosang likarolo.
 
- Setšoantšo se hlahisoa bakeng sa leihlo le leng le le leng ka thoko - ke boko feela bo se kopanyang ho ba setšoantšo se le seng.
 
- Setšoantšo se bohale karolong e nyenyane feela bohareng 'me se ba sefifi ha se ea mahlakoreng.
 
- Setšoantšo se na le mebala e mengata karolong e nyenyane bohareng 'me se lahleheloa ke ho hlaka ha se ea mahlakoreng. Sena se bakoa ke palo e phahameng ea li-cones bohareng ba leihlo, tse fokotsehang ka potlako ho tloha bohareng. Leihlong le leng le le leng ho na le letheba le letšo - le bitsoang sebaka se sa boneng (scotoma). Karolong ena ea leihlo ha ho na li-cones kapa li-rods, hobane ke moo methapo ea leihlo e leng teng. Boko bo tloaelehile ho tlatsa libaka tsena tse sa boneng ka boitsebiso bo tsoang leihlong le leng.
 
- Mela e otlolohileng e sothehile hanyane ho ba mookoli joalokaha eka ke phello ea leihlo la tlhapi kapa lense ea khamera e pharaletseng. Boko bo leka ho otlolla mela ena, empa ke thetso feela.
 
- Nko e boetse e bonahala tšimong ea pono, eo boko bo tloaelehileng ho e sefa.
 
- Setšoantšo ha se hloekile ka ho feletseng, empa se na le lerata le fokolang la pono. Sena se bakoa ke motsoako oa lerata la 'mele la leseli, lerata la li-photoreceptors ka botsona le mesebetsi e meng ea methapo ea tsamaiso ea pono. Tlas'a maemo a tloaelehileng, boko bo atisa ho e hatella, empa e ka bonahala haholo lefifing, ha mahlo a koetsoe kapa holim'a libaka tse tšoanang.
 
 
Bakeng sa mosebetsi oa ka oa selemo sa bobeli oa thuto ea multimedia ho tsoa thutong ea animation Sekolong sa Khoebo, Bonono bo Sebelisitsoeng le Moralo, ke entse video e etsisang mafu a fapaneng a thahasellisang a mahlo:
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/sTMWRaVGFIE]

Tanzania 2019
 
Lifoto, mongolo
 
 
 
Tlhahiso ena ke kakaretso ya ditlhokomelo le tsebo tse fapaneng tseo ke di kgobokantseng nakong ya ha ke ne ke le Tanzania nako e kgutshwane. Ke hlalosa ditlhokomelo le maikutlo ana ka lifoto le ka mongolo.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-3.jpg]

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-4.jpg] 

 
Ka mosebetsi wa ka, ke leka ho thusa baahi ba moo, ho phatlalatsa tlhokomediso mabapi le mathata a bona le ho felisa kgethollo ya morabe. Hape ke dumela hore Europe e lokela ho nka mohlala ho naha ena dinthong tse ngata.
 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-5.jpg]

Tanzania ho na le dihlopha tse pedi tse kgolo tsa bodumedi - Boislamo le Bokreste. Le hoja ho phela mmoho ha dihlopha tsena tse pedi ho ka ba thata ho motho wa Europe ya tloaelehileng ho ho nahana ho tloha lekholong la 21, Mamosleme le Bakreste ba moo ba phela ka kgotso mme ba hlomphana.

Ka seqo, ha ke rate batho ba apereng junifomo (mapolesa, masole, balebedi...). bonyane eseng naheng ya Czech. Ke dumela hore ka matla ao ba a filweng, boikgohomoso ba bona bo a hola. Leha ho le jwalo, ho hana ha ka junifomo ho ne ho se opodstatněný.Lid ka ho feletseng Tanzania. Batho ba apereng junifomo ba ne ba ntsheha hantle mme ba ne ba dula ba nthusa.
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-11.jpg]

Baahi ba moo ba ntsheha e le batho ba amohelang baeti le ba botsoalle, esita le kantle ho dibaka tsa bahahlauli. Ha ke ne ke etela motseng wa moo, batho ba ne ba nkamogela ka tumediso ya "Jambo!" (e bolelang "Dumela!"). Ba bangata ba bona ba ile ba buisana le nna.

[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-22.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-23.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-24.jpg] 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-25.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-26.jpg] 

 
"Batho bana ba tswang dibakeng tsena tse futhumetseng ba bonahala ba bososela ho feta rona" (karolo ya moqoqo wa bapalami ba mmalwa oo ke o utlwileng sefofaneng ha ke kgutlela Czech Republic)
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-27.jpg] 
 
Ka lebaka la kgwebisano ya lefatshe lohle, ho na le bohahlauli bo hlahang Zanzibar, bo tlisang melemo e mengata, empa hape le mathata. Melemo e meholo ke kgolo ya moruo le boemo bo ntseng bo hola ba thuto. Molemo o mong ke ho eketsa tlhokomediso ka naha ena lefatsheng, empa ka maswabi molemo ona hangata o hlakolwa ke marako a maholo a dihotele ao bahahlauli ba bangata ba sa a tloleng le ka mohla. Ke nka ho senngwa ha setso sa moo e le bothata. Mohlala wa bothata bona ke ho falla ha Masai ba bangata ba tswang Tanzania (haholo-holo sebakeng se potapotileng Kilimanjaro) ho ya dibakeng tse nang le bahahlauli ba bangata (mabopong a Zanzibar). Masai ana a rekisa dintho tse nyane mona mme ke ntho feela ya ho hohela bahahlauli. Masai ba dumela hore haeba motho a ba nka lifoto, o utswa moya wa bona. Leha ho le jwalo, dibakeng tsa bahahlauli, ba itumella ho nkuwa lifoto bakeng sa lisente tse mmalwa.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/cestovani-tanzanie-2019-obr-28.jpg]

SLUG: portret-osobnosti

SOURCE: src/content/posts/portret-osobnosti.mdx

============================================================
Setšoantšo sa motho
 
 Tshitwe 2018 
 
 
 
Morero ona o hlahile e le mosebetsi oa sekolo bakeng sa thuto ea Lifoto Sekolong se Phahameng sa Khoebo, Bonono bo Sebetsang le Moralo se Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Sepheo e ne e le ho kopanya ka tsela ea liteko lifoto tsa setšoantšo sa motho, mefuta ea botho ea kelello, projekšene ea litšoantšo, boitukiso le molumo. Sebopeho sa ho qetela sa lifoto le livideo se thehiloe holim'a teko ea botho ea Eysenck le karohano ea mefuta ea boitšoaro ho latela sehlopha sa Galen.

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-3.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/portret-osobnosti-obr-4.jpg]

TITLE: Sebopeho sa chekerboto
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Sebopeho sa chekerboto
 
 Tshitwe 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Ke sebelisitse sebopeho sa chekerboto setšoantšong sa mohlala, moo tšimo ka nngwe ea chekerboto e lekanang le pixel e le nngwe ea senepe. Libaka tse tšoeu tsa tšimo ea chekerboto ka mor'a ho sebelisa sebopeho sena ke li-pixel tsa pele tsa senepe, 'me libaka tse ntšo ke tse fapaneng ka 'mala le khanya. Senepe se boholo bo felletseng se shebahala joaloka sebaka se seputsoa se le seng, empa ke ka mor'a ho atametsa ka dijithale moo senepe sa pele se hlahang teng. Bakeng sa pontšo e nepahetseng ho leihlo la Full HD, senepe se tlameha ho ba le bophahamo ba hoo e ka bang 2000 px. Pontšo e fosahetseng e ka etsahala ha u bula senepe ho sebatli se etsang bicubic resampling ea grid ea pixel. Sebatli se netefalitsoeng ke mohlala, Windows Photo Viewer.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Joaloka mohlala o mong oa tšebeliso ea molao-motheo ona, ke thehile senepe se nang le phapang ea 'mala le moelelo.

============================================================
Ho sotha tlhahisoleseding
 
 phato 2018 
 
 
 
Ke ka baahi ba bakae ba mahareng bao sehlooho seo re se bonang inthaneteng se fetang ho fihla ho rona? Na 'nete ea sona e ka tšeptjoa? Lefatše leo re le bonang ka li-skrineng tsa rona tsa mehala, likhomphutha le thelevishene le fapane hakae?
 
Tlhahisoleseding e 'ngoe le e 'ngoe eo re e bonang ke ea bohlokoa ho e netefatsa mehloling e mengata 'me re se ke ra itšetleha ka 'nete ea eona.
 
Mosebetsi ona o entsoe ka ho nka senepe se le seng ebe o nka linepe tse pheta-phetoang tsa senepe sona seo ho tsoa ho sebali sa khomphutha.

TITLE: Meqho e mebedi
SLUG: dva-rytmy
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/dva-rytmy/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/dva-rytmy.mdx
BUILT_HTML: dva-rytmy/index.html
============================================================
Meqho e mebedi
 
 Apole 2018 
 
 
 
Video sa pele se entsoe ka meqho e mebedi: ho panya ha pono le ho penya ha modumo. Meqho ena e mebedi e na le nako e fapaneng hanyenyane. Ka phapang ena e nyane, video e fihlela boemo ba ho se phutholohe haholo karolong e itseng. Videong ea bobeli, ke kentse thepa ea litšoantšo e shebaneng le lihlooho tsa khahlanong le ntoa ho mohopolo ona oa mantlha.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/X9ms8pp89Pg?rel=0&showinfo=0]

TITLE: Dipalo tse sa reroang tse kgethilweng ke motho
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Dipalo tse sa reroang tse kgethilweng ke motho
 
 Aperele 2018 
 
 
 
Mosebetsing ona, ho ile ha abjwa lethathamo la dipotso ho batho ba mmalwa. Batho ba neng ba lekolwa ba ile ba ngola dipalo tse lekgetho tse lekgolo tse sa reroang tse tswang ho 1-100, tseo ba neng ba nahana ka tsona. Ka mora moo, ke ile ka fetisetsa dintlha tsena khomphuteng mme ka etsa kerafo e le nngwe ho tswa ho lethathamo le leng le le leng la dipotso. Kerafo ya x e rapameng e bontsha tatellano ya dipalo tse fapaneng mme kerafo ya y e otlolohileng e bontsha boleng ba dipalo tse kgethilweng. Kerafo ya mmala o moputswa e entswe ke khomphuta, ke hore, ke jenereithara ya dipalo tse sa reroang. Ha ho bapiswa dikerafo tsohle, motho a ka hlokomela hore motho e mong le e mong ya lekolwang o na le kerafo e ikgethang e nang le mokgwa wa hae wa ho kenya dipalo. Hape ho ka hlokomelwa hore dikerafo tse ngata di pheta dipotoloho tse batlang di tshwana (haholo-holo ka mokgwa wa ho eketsa le ho fokotsa dipalo hanyane ka hanyane).
 
DIPALO TSE KGETHILWENG KE JENEREITHARA YA DIPALO TSE SA REROANG

 hlakubele 2018

TITLE: Ho tswa ponong ya kgapetla
SLUG: z-pohledu-musle
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/z-pohledu-musle/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/z-pohledu-musle.mdx
BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Ho tswa ponong ya kgapetla
 
 hllakubele 2018 
 
 
 
Dividiyo tsena di shebile temoho ya kgapetla, hore e ikutlwang le hore e na le phihlelo e jwang? Pono ya yona ya lefatshe ha e a tlase ka bohlokwa ho feta ya motho! Videong ya pele, motho a ka bona rekoto ya pele ya lewatle. Videong ya bobedi, ka ho le letshehadi la skrine, ho hlahiswa mmala o tloaelehileng wa diforeimi ka nngwe tsa rekoto ya pele. Ka ho le letona la skrine, ho na le kerafo ya RGB e hlalosang mmala ona.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/mxfAcE8KYaQ?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lZknZa20ilY?rel=0&showinfo=0]

TITLE: Hlobo
SLUG: plisen

============================================================
Hlobo
 
 Pulungoana 2017 
 
 
 
Potoloho ena ea linepe e ngola hlobo e bukeng ea ka ea puo ea Sejeremane. Linepe li hlophisitsoe ka tatellano ea kamoo maqephe a buka a latellanang kateng. Sena se etsa hore ho be le meqapi e sa reroang, e khahlisang le ea liteko ka har'a senepe se seng le se seng.

TITLE: Tshitiso sa gridi ea kamehla
SLUG: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-10-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Tshitiso sa gridi ea kamehla
 
 Mphalane 2017 
 
 
 
Mosebetsing ona ke lekile ho theha letoto la mesebetsi ea bonono e sa bonahaleng. Ke arotse tokomane ena likarolong tse nne. Karolong ea pele, ke thehile gridi ea mantlha ea kamehla, karolong ea bobeli ke qalile ho tloha ho gridi ena mme ka qala ho e sitisa hanyane, e leng se bakileng ho se phutholohe. Karolong ea boraro, ke sitisitse gridi ena ea jiometri ea kamehla ka libopeho tse sa tloaelehang tsa tlhaho tse fapaneng. Mme karolong ea ho qetela ea bone, ke entse hore libopeho tsena tsa tlhaho li tsamaee.
 
Karolo ea pele:
 GRIDI EA MANTLHA
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Abstraction 1.0 - gridi ea kamehla]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
Karolo ea bobeli:
 TŠITISO EA GRIDI KA PHAPANG E NYANE
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-2.jpg | Abstraction 2.0.0 - gridi ea kamehla e sitisitsoeng ke mola o le mong o potolohileng]

Tsoho
 
 Mmesa 2017 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-1.jpg | senepe sa mantlha se sa fetolwang]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-1.jpg]

Karolong e ka ho le letona la setšoantšo ke averaje ea mebala ea lipiksiele tsohle ho tsoa karolong ea letsoho le letšehali, ke hore, averaje ea mebala ea dinepe tsohle ho tsoa naheng eo.

Dikhoto tsa tswekere
 
 Hlakubele 2017 
 
 
 
Letoto la dinepe le hlahile nakong ya diteko tsa talenta Sekolong se Phahameng sa Kgwebo, Bonono bo Sebediswang le Moralo se Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Bakeng sa karolo ya dinepe ya diteko re ne re ka tlisa dintho tsa rona, empa palo le boholo ba tsona di ne di lekanyeditswe. Ke tlisitse lebokose la dikhoto tsa tswekere, tseo ke neng ke di hlophisa poleiting ka mokgwa o hlophisehileng mme ka moraho ka di tshela ka metsi. Dikhoto di ile tsa qhibidiha butle-butle, tsa wa mme tsa wela ka tsela e sa lebelloang.

TITLE: Laeborari ya bonono ba seterateng
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Laeborari ya bonono ba seterateng
 
 Hlakubele 2017 
 
 
 
Ho pota-pota rona ho na le mesebetsi e mengata e thahasellisang ya bonono ba seterateng ho tloha ho ditikara tse nyane, mabota a pentiloeng ho isa mefuteng e fapaneng ya 3D dintho. Kaha bangodi ba dipontsho tsena ba tlameha ho dula ba sa tsebahale ka lebaka la ho se utlwane le molao, lefatshe lena la bonono ba seterateng le se na boithati ebile le arohane le lefatshe la kgwebo. Bonono ba seterateng boo re ka bo bonang hoo e ka bang hohle toropong bo dula bo le bocha. Bonono bo botjha ba seterateng bo kwahela ba kgale, pente e a qhibidika mmoho le polasetara, dintho tsa 3D di senngwa ke batho mme ditikara di a tloha kapa di fela ka lebaka la maemo a lehodimo ha nako e ntse e ya. Ke tshebetso ya tlhaho, ntlha ya nako, empa ke nahana hore bonono bo bong ba seterateng bo lokela ho atlehiswa bophelo ba bona.
 
Ke ka lebaka leo ke entseng qeto ya ho ngola mesebetsi e thahasellisang ka ho fetisisa. Bakeng sa morero ona, ka selemo sa 2017 ke thehile leqephe la Facebook le bitsoang Street art library, leo ke ntseng ke kenya ditaba ho lona ho fihlela kajeno. E le ho fihlela batho ba bangata, ke ile ka theha leqephe la lebitso le le leng ho Instagram.
 
Maqepheng ana ke leka ho kenya haholo mesebetsi e nang le mohopolo o itseng mme karolo ya botle ba mosebetsi ke e nka e le ya bohlokwa ba bobedi.
 
Laeborari ya bonono ba seterateng (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Laeborari ya bonono ba seterateng (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Senepe sa skrini sa leqephe la Facebook]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Senepe sa skrini sa leqephe la Instagram]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
Ha ho tsotellehe hore o tswa seterekeng sefe...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
Ka 28. Tshitwe 2014 ke ile ka nka senepe sa lebota le kgale le ngodilweng ho lona motseng wa Mombasa. Ka mora moo, ke ile ka leka ho hlalosa mongolo o ho lona. Karolo e nngwe e balehang ke ile ka e kenya enjeneng ya ho batla ya inthanete mme ka fumana senepe se latelang.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
Tlaleho e tswang ho Plzeň TV:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

TITLE: Ho tloha motseng ho fihla ho monyetla
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2016-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Ho tloha motseng ho fihla ho monyetla
 
 Pherekgong 2016 
 
 
 
Ka selemo sa 2016 ke ile ka fuoa mosebetsi oa ho etsa setšoantšo sa toropo e le karolo ea boitokisetso ba sekolo sa bonono sa ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14. Ke ne ke batla ho tseba hore na setšoantšo sena se ka fihla hokae, kahoo ke ile ka sebetsa mosebetsing ona ho tloha lilemong tse latelang ho fihlela kajeno (selemo sa 2019). Ho tloha ho setšoantšo se bonolo sa toropo, ke ile ka fihla ho senyeheng ha li-grid tse tloaelehileng, tse bakileng tsitsipano e makatsang ho latela pono ea bonono. Ho feta moo, ke lekile ho sebelisa monyetla mosebetsing ona, empa ha kea ka ka khona ho fumana letho le kang monyetla oa sebele ntle le sesosa. Determinism e ne e lula e nthibela, kahoo ke ile ka sebelisa feela pseudo-randomness (sesosa se ka khetholloa, empa sephetho se thata ho boleloa esale pele, kahoo se bonahala eka ke monyetla). Litsela tse ling li ile tsa ntlisa tseleng e sa tsoeleng pele, kahoo ke ile ka tlameha ho batla pono e ncha le tharollo e ncha ea litšoantšo, mohlala, ho hlophisa tlhahiso ea pono, phello ea moiré, tatellano ea Fibonacci, joalo-joalo. Letoto la litšoantšo tse ka tlase ke kakaretso ea mesebetsi ena.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg | Mombasa: senepe sa mongoli le litšoantšo tsa hae]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-2.jpg | Setšoantšo sa toropo: karohano ea libaka]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-2.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-3.jpg | Setšoantšo sa toropo: ho senyeha ha grid e tloaelehileng]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-3.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-4.jpg | Setšoantšo sa toropo: ho senyeha ha sebopeho sa jiometri se tloaelehileng]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-4.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-5.jpg | Setšoantšo sa toropo: ho senyeha ha grid e tloaelehileng ka ho potoloha ka tšohanyetso]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-5.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-6.jpg | Setšoantšo sa toropo: sebopeho sa tšohanyetso tšimong ea likhokahano]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-6.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-7.jpg | Setšoantšo sa toropo: libopeho tse bonolo le ho tsamaea ka tšohanyetso]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-7.jpg]

TITLE: Mofoka
SLUG: plevel
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/plevel/
LANGUAGE: st
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2008-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/plevel.mdx
BUILT_HTML: plevel/index.html
============================================================
Mofoka
 
 Motsheanong 2008 
 
 
 
Mosebetsi o tswang sekolong sa bana ba banyenyane.
 
Taelo e ne e le ho seha dipalesa tse thunyang pampiring e mebala-bala le ho etsa setshwantsho ka tsona.
 
Ho ena le dipalesa, ke entse plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
